# Historical names delivered at research time, not at a bookmark.
# The bookmark dispatcher can only name a tier a bookmark date reaches, so these historical
# names for later tiers were undeliverable. Each helper below is called from its chassis
# tier's own technology via on_research_complete in NSB_armor.txt, and names the design for
# the researching country. Recipes and name provenance are machine-checked against
# LogDocs/Tank_Designer/data/Research_Naming_Manifest.json.

cwic_name_heavy_tank_artillery_chassis_4_variants = {
	# CUB: CUB_heavy_sp_artillery_equipment_4 in CUB_equipment_l_english.yml:157
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_dlc = "No Step Back"
			tag = CUB
			NOT = { has_country_flag = cwic_named_heavy_tank_artillery_chassis_4_created }
		}
		create_equipment_variant = {
			name = "T-130"
			type = heavy_tank_artillery_chassis_4
			allow_without_tech = yes
			parent_version = 0
			mark_older_equipment_obsolete = yes
			modules = {
				main_armament_slot = tank_low_p_cannon0
				turret_type_slot = heavy_open_gun
				suspension_type_slot = Torsion_1
				armor_type_slot = Armor_1_Comp
				engine_type_slot = Diesel_1
				tank_special_slot_1 = ap_2p
				tank_special_slot_2 = tank_he_1p
				tank_special_slot_3 = Aim_2
				tank_special_slot_4 = Optics_2
				tank_special_slot_5 = Computer_0
				tank_special_slot_12 = Smoke_ESS
				tank_special_slot_13 = FFS_1
				tank_special_slot_14 = APU_1
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = cwic_named_heavy_tank_artillery_chassis_4_created
	}
